SSH 到 rtx5090-4x\n2. 了解 generation pool 的数据结构：\n   ```bash\n   source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\n   # 检查 generation pool\n   ls /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/\n   # 检查单个文件格式\n   head -1 /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/*.jsonl | python -c \"import json,sys; d=json.loads(sys.stdin.readline()); print(list(d.keys()))\"\n   # 统计总样本数\n   wc -l /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/*.jsonl\n   ```\n\n3. 写脚本 `/root/autodl-tmp/scripts/sample_topk3_random.py`：\n   ```python\n   \"\"\"\n   从完整 generation pool 中随机抽取 8037 样本（固定 seed=42）。\n   不使用任何 scoring，纯随机选择。\n   输出格式与 topk3 训练集一致。\n   \"\"\"\n   ```\n   \n   脚本逻辑：\n   - 读取所有 generation 文件（`/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/*.jsonl`）\n   - 过滤掉 f1_reward < 0.1 的样本（与 CED scoring 保持相同的最低质量门槛，否则对照不公平）\n   - 从剩余样本中随机抽取 8037 个（seed=42）\n   - 输出到 `/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered_topk3_random/rsft_train.jsonl`\n   - 输出格式必须与 `/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered_topk3/rsft_train.jsonl` 完全一致（相同字段结构）\n   - 打印统计：总 pool 大小、过滤后大小、抽样数、mean_f1、mean_ced（如果有 ced 字段）\n\n4. 运行脚本并记录结果\n\n5. 验证输出：\n   ```bash\n   wc -l /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered_topk3_random/rsft_train.jsonl\n   head -1 /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered_topk3_random/rsft_train.jsonl | python -m json.tool | head -5\n   ```\n\n### 注意\n- 检查 generation pool 中的样本是否已经包含 f1_reward/ced_score 字段。如果有，直接用；如果没有，需要从 rsft_scores.jsonl 关联\n- generation pool 可能有多个 shard 文件（s0, s1, s2, s3）\n- 随机抽样需要先合并所有 shard 再抽样\n- seed=42 固定，确保可复现\n\n### 环境\n- 服务器: rtx5090-4x\n- conda: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`\n- CPU 任务，不占 GPU\n"

&lt;project_context>
# 项目: faith-evidence-ie

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: rtx5090-4x
- SSH 账户: root（调用 ssh_execute / submit_training_job 时传入 account="root"）
- SSH 连接命令: ssh rtx5090-4x  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/autodl-tmp/freige


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
## 任务：生成 topk3_random 对照数据集

### 背景
plan_008 ablation 改为 topk3_ced (8037) vs topk3_random (8037)。需要从完整 generation pool 中随机抽取 8037 样本作为对照组，回答"CED scoring 是否比随机选择更好"。

之前的 CED > 0 过滤方案已废弃（仅移除 1.2% 样本，无区分度）。

### 你的任务

1. SSH 到 rtx5090-4x
2. 了解 generation pool 的数据结构：
   ```bash
   source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base
   # 检查 generation pool
   ls /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/
   # 检查单个文件格式
   head -1 /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/*.jsonl | python -c "import json,sys; d=json.loads(sys.stdin.readline()); print(list(d.keys()))"
   # 统计总样本数
   wc -l /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/*.jsonl
   ```

3. 写脚本 `/root/autodl-tmp/scripts/sample_topk3_random.py`：
   ```python
   """
   从完整 generation pool 中随机抽取 8037 样本（固定 seed=42）。
   不使用任何 scoring，纯随机选择。
   输出格式与 topk3 训练集一致。
   """
   ```
   
   脚本逻辑：
   - 读取所有 generation 文件（`/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/*.jsonl`）
   - 过滤掉 f1_reward < 0.1 的样本（与 CED scoring 保持相同的最低质量门槛，否则对照不公平）
   - 从剩余样本中随机抽取 8037 个（seed=42）
   - 输出到 `/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered_topk3_random/rsft_train.jsonl`
   - 输出格式必须与 `/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered_topk3/rsft_train.jsonl` 完全一致（相同字段结构）
   - 打印统计：总 pool 大小、过滤后大小、抽样数、mean_f1、mean_ced（如果有 ced 字段）

4. 运行脚本并记录结果

5. 验证输出：
   ```bash
   wc -l /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered_topk3_random/rsft_train.jsonl
   head -1 /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered_topk3_random/rsft_train.jsonl | python -m json.tool | head -5
   ```

### 注意
- 检查 generation pool 中的样本是否已经包含 f1_reward/ced_score 字段。如果有，直接用；如果没有，需要从 rsft_scores.jsonl 关联
- generation pool 可能有多个 shard 文件（s0, s1, s2, s3）
- 随机抽样需要先合并所有 shard 再抽样
- seed=42 固定，确保可复现

### 环境
- 服务器: rtx5090-4x
- conda: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`
- CPU 任务，不占 GPU

Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. })\\n\\n    return sft_data\\n\\n\\ndef _extract_user_content(prompt_text):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"从完整 prompt 中提取 user 消息内容。\\n\\n    prompt 是 apply_chat_template 的输出，包含 system/user 标记。\\n 3. 筛选\\n    selected, filter_stats = filter_records(scored, args)\\n\\n    # 4. 转换为 SFT 训练格式\\n    sft_data = format_sft_training_data(selected)\\n    train_path = output_dir / \\\"rsft_train.jsonl\\\"\\n    with open(train_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        for item in sft_data:\\n            f.write(json.dumps(item, ensure_ascii=False) + \\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n    logger.info(\\\"Training data → %s (%d samples)\\\", train_path, len(sft_data))\\n\\n    # 5. 统计报告\\n    report = generate_report(scored, selected)\\n    report[\\\"f1_filter_stats\\\"] = filter_stats\\n    report[\\\"truncation_analysis\\\"] = truncation_analysis\\n    report[\\\"args\\\"] = vars(args)\\n    report_path = output_dir / \\\"rsft_report.json\\\"\\n    with open(report_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\\n    logger.info(\\\"Report → %s\\\", report_path)\\n\\n    # 控制台摘要\\n    print(\\\"\\\\n\\\" + \\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n    print(f\\\"  Total records:    {report['total_records']}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Selected:         {report['selected_records']}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Selection rate:   {report['selection_rate']:.1%}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Unique docs:      {report['unique_docs_selected']}/{report['unique_docs_total']}\\\")\\n    print(\\\"-\\\" * 60)\\n    for name in [\\\"format_reward\\\", \\\"f1_reward\\\", \\\"ced_reward\\\", \\\"combined_score\\\"]:\\n        stats = report[f\\\"{name}_stats\\\"]\\n        print(f\\\"  {name:20s}  mean={stats['mean']:.3f}  std={stats['std']:.3f}  \\\"\\n              f\\\"median={stats['median']:.3f}  [min={stats['min']:.3f}, max={stats['max']:.3f}]\\\")\\n    print(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.38}}"

Now I understand the full picture:

**Data sources:**
- `generations.jsonl` (21968): raw generations with `prompt`, `output`, `gold_triples`, `sents` etc.
- `rsft_scored_s0-s3/rsft_scores.jsonl` (21952 total): scored records with `f1_reward`, `ced_reward`, `combined_score` but WITHOUT `prompt`, `sents`, `gold_triples` (stripped in scoring to save space)
- `rsft_filtered_topk3/rsft_train.jsonl` (8037): training format with `input` (extracted from prompt), `output`, `ced_score`, `doc_id`, `generation_idx`, `format_reward`, `f1_reward`, `combined_score`


- `ced_score` maps to `ced_reward` and `input` is the extracted user content from `prompt`

For the sampling script, I'll load all scored shards to get the reward metrics and output, then join with the generations file to retrieve the original prompts, filter for f1_reward >= 0.1, and randomly sample 8 records.

The scored files strip large fields like `prompt` to save space, so I need to join by (doc_id, generation_idx) with generations.jsonl to get that data back. There's a slight mismatch in record counts (21968 vs 21952), but the join should handle that. Now I'll write the script to pull this together.

{
  "server": "rtx5090-4x",
  "command": "cat > /root/autodl-tmp/scripts/sample_topk3_random.py << 'PYEOF'\n\"\"\"\n从完整 generation pool 中随机抽取 8037 样本（固定 seed=42）。\n不使用任何 scoring 排序，纯随机选择。\n输出格式与 topk3 训练集一致。\n\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport random\nimport os\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nSEED = 42\nTARGET_N = 8037\nF1_THRESHOLD = 0.1\n\nSCORE_SHARDS = [\n    \"/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_scored_s0/rsft_scores.jsonl\",\n    \"/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_scored_s1/rsft_scores.jsonl\",\n    \"/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_scored_s2/rsft_scores.jsonl\",\n    \"/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_scored_s3/rsft_scores.jsonl\",\n]\nGENERATIONS_PATH = \"/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/generations.jsonl\"\nOUTPUT_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered_topk3_random\"\nOUTPUT_PATH = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"rsft_train.jsonl\")\n\n\ndef extract_user_content(prompt_text):\n    user_start = prompt_text.find(\"<|im_start|>user\\n\")\n    if user_start < 0:\n        return prompt_text\n    content_start = user_start + len(\"<|im_start|>user\\n\")\n    user_end = prompt_text.find(\"<|im_end|>\", content_start)\n    if user_end < 0:\n        return prompt_text[content_start:]\n    return prompt_text[content_start:user_end]\n\n\ndef main():\n    # 1. Load scored shards\n    print(\"Loading scored shards...\")\n    scored = {}\n    for path in SCORE_SHARDS:\n        with open(path) as f:\n            for line in f:\n                rec = json.loads(line)\n                key = (rec[\"doc_id\"], rec[\"generation_idx\"])\n                scored[key] = rec\n    print(f\"  Total scored: {len(scored)}\")\n\n    # 2. Load generations (for prompt field)\n    print(\"Loading generations (for prompt)...\")\n    prompts = {}\n    with open(GENERATIONS_PATH) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            rec = json.loads(line)\n            key = (rec[\"doc_id\"], rec[\"generation_idx\"])\n            prompts[key] = rec[\"prompt\"]\n    print(f\"  Total generations: {len(prompts)}\")\n\n    # 3. Merge and filter\n    merged = []\n    missing_prompt = 0\n    for key, srec in scored.items():\n        if key not in prompts:\n            missing_prompt += 1\n            continue\n        if srec.get(\"f1_reward\", 0) < F1_THRESHOLD:\n            continue\n        merged.append({\n            \"input\": extract_user_content(prompts[key]),\n            \"output\": srec[\"output\"],\n            \"ced_score\": srec.get(\"ced_reward\", 0.0),\n            \"doc_id\": srec[\"doc_id\"],\n            \"generation_idx\": srec[\"generation_idx\"],\n            \"format_reward\": srec.get(\"format_reward\", 0.0),\n            \"f1_reward\": srec[\"f1_reward\"],\n            \"combined_score\": srec.get(\"combined_score\", 0.0),\n        })\n\n    print(f\"  Missing prompt (no join): {missing_prompt}\")\n    print(f\"  After f1 >= {F1_THRESHOLD} filter: {len(merged)}\")\n\n    if len(merged) < TARGET_N:\n        print(f\"  WARNING: pool ({len(merged)}) < target ({TARGET_N}), using all\")\n        sampled = merged\n    else:\n        random.seed(SEED)\n        sampled = random.sample(merged, TARGET_N)\n\n    # 4. Stats\n    f1s = [s[\"f1_reward\"] for s in sampled]\n    ceds = [s[\"ced_score\"] for s in sampled]\n    mean_f1 = sum(f1s) / len(f1s)\n    mean_ced = sum(ceds) / len(ceds)\n    unique_docs = len(set(s[\"doc_id\"] for s in sampled))\n\n    print(f\"\\n=== Results ===\")\n    print(f\"  Pool size (all scored):     {len(scored)}\")\n    print(f\"  After f1 >= {F1_THRESHOLD}:          {len(merged)}\")\n    print(f\"  Sampled:                    {len(sampled)}\")\n    print(f\"  Unique docs:                {unique_docs}\")\n    print(f\"  Mean f1_reward:             {mean_f1:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"  Mean ced_score:             {mean_ced:.4f}\")\n\n    # 5. Write output\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(OUTPUT_PATH, \"w\") as f:\n        for item in sampled:\n            f.write(json.dumps(item, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n    print(f\"\\n  Output → {OUTPUT_PATH} ({len(sampled)} samples)\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nPYEOF",
  "account": "root"
}
